## Wanted sources

WANTED: Illusie, On the semistable reduction and the calculation of nearby cycles (2004), Theorem 1.4 and its proof, for lem-per-semistable-coordinate-kummer-tower-acyclic.
WANTED: Rapoport–Zink, Über die lokale Zetafunktion von Shimuravarietäten (1982), Satz 2.21 and its proof, for lem-per-semistable-coordinate-kummer-tower-acyclic.

ILO is present and read. Neither original is in literature/LIST.md's obtained table. No document was downloaded. A web bibliographic lookup only confirmed the original reference; it was not used as a proof. The item is therefore marked proof_origin.by=agent. The tower-acyclicity formulation is explicitly an agent input to be proved, not attributed verbatim to an unread theorem. It allows smooth coordinates so its later proof need not repeat the chart setup. Its statement has no purity conclusion or fundamental-class map: it asks that classes at finite stages die at higher Kummer-root stages. The item's argument carries torsor descent, the cohomology computation, localization, and the identification of the actual Cartier map. It does not apply a renamed coordinate-divisor purity theorem.

## Review by Q63

I read ILO XVI Definition 3.5.11, Proposition 3.5.12 and Lemma 3.5.13(iv), printed pp.250–251, including the actual paragraph referring to Illusie Theorem 1.4 and Rapoport–Zink Satz 2.21. The item asks for each individual coordinate Cartier map, whereas the monomial parent's conclusion is punctual purity. I kept the parent's existing route: none of the monomial-purity, log-smooth-purity or absolute-purity conclusions is cited back into this input. There is no excellence assumption hidden in a completion step.

For the new geometric input I checked the trait, invertibility, index ranges, coordinate being in the geometric closed-point maximal ideal, smooth parameters, and transition u_r = u_(r+1)^ell. The assertion is the single-coordinate Kummer-tower cohomology-erasure formulation of the missing semistable calculation; I do not claim to have read its proof in the unavailable originals. It is not marked proved or Mathlib. For the new descent input I read Stacks sites-cohomology.tex, Tag 03AZ, lemma-cech-spectral-sequence and its proof, and the nearby sheaf-set Cech discussion; the finite torsor Cech terms are products indexed by powers of G_r. Passing to the filtered system yields continuous cochains with M^b = colim H^b(V_r,A). This is a genuine tower comparison beyond the finite-cover node, so it remains a statement-only lemma, explicitly marked agent-formulated. Its H1 edge normalization is the transition cocycle of the first torsor. I also read the existing procyclic node and reused it, instead of creating another group-cohomology lemma.

Step 1 reads the full statement of primitive-monomial-trait-geometry: all-one exponents satisfy its fraction-field invertibility hypothesis, so it gives the domain, regularity and Noetherianity actually used. Injection of R follows by polynomial total degree; the displayed quotient by T_i is computed directly. No separate “Cartier coordinate” node was created.

Step 2 reads the closed-immersion strict-localization Cartesian statement, not a mere definition of strict localization. It identifies D as the strict localization of H_i. The class-calculus statement explicitly includes strict-local compatibility; the sections/stalk node gives the germ isomorphism and the strict-henselian acyclicity node gives exactness for every abelian sheaf. Thus their use for derived cohomology stalks is justified without claiming that a single geometric stalk is conservative. Final conservativity is used only after every geometric point has been treated.

Step 3 uses the new geometric node only for H0 at all stages and the vanishing direct limits. The strict-local roots-of-unity statement gives cyclic groups of the correct order and unit differences; successive primitive roots can be chosen with prescribed ell-th powers because a primitive root at stage r+1 maps to a primitive root at r, and powers adjust it. The standard-etale-monic-derivative node applies on C[1/t_i] because ell and u_r are units. The monic quotient is finite free of positive rank, hence each geometric fibre is nonempty. For the torsor self-product, factor z^(ell^r)-1 into its pairwise-comaximal linear factors and use the Chinese remainder formula. This is written inline, not hidden in a nonexistent root-torsor node.

Step 4 checks every descent hypothesis: V is the affine principal open in a strict localization, every root cover is a cyclic finite etale surjective torsor, and transition maps reduce the acting group. Constant H0 makes the coefficient action trivial. The existing procyclic-two-term statement applies with A=F_ell and exponent s=1; its differential is zero for this action. The descent node's last sentence and torsor-H1's actual Cech normalization identify the generator with the first root torsor. The exact Kummer sequence identifies that torsor with delta(t_i), rather than merely postulating an abstract H1 isomorphism.

Step 5 uses the exact displayed cone formula in closed-support-hom, with coefficients A(1). Roots chosen in C trivialize that line, so the strict-henselian acyclicity node applies. H0(Y) to H0(V) is the identity on constant sections. The cone long exact sequence gives precisely one supported group, in degree two, and its generator is the image of delta(t_i). There is no assertion of acyclicity on V before the tower calculation.

Step 6 uses ordinary-supported-kummer's specified relative Cartier boundary and the full supported-boundary definition (Yoneda product with the actual Kummer extension), not just the statement c_D u_D = c1(O(D)), which alone would not determine the supported class. The explicit frame 1/t_i and its transition t_i identify the class with the boundary just computed. A dual convention changes only its unit sign, also in characteristic two. Thus the actual map is an isomorphism on each stalk; the derived-stalk-conservative statement gives the global isomorphism.

I tested n=1 (the ordinary trait and its residue point), n=2 at the crossing, points where other T_j are invertible, m=0 and extra smooth coordinates in the new input, ell=2, equal and mixed characteristic with ell invertible, and traits which are not excellent, complete or henselian. The tower assertion deliberately inverts only T_i; no all-coordinate puncture is silently substituted. The descent statement includes split torsor towers: in that case H0(V_r) is not A, so only its general spectral sequence applies, not the acyclic specialization. This excludes a false connectedness assumption from the generic statement.

B–E self-check: new nodes introduce all objects and hypotheses, use TeX, cite their notions, and carry no proof or proof_deps. Their source places distinguish agent formulations from verbatim source statements. Searches were made with msearch.py and all hits were read; two semantic phrasings precede no-match conclusions. Loogle's unresolved lowercase etale query is recorded as unresolved, not evidence of absence. A later Kummer query resolves and returns only algebraic number-theory statements. The Mathlib GroupCohomology/Basic.lean TODO at lines 59–65 still lists profinite cohomology and Hochschild–Serre; finite-cyclic resolution signatures do not compare scheme cohomology. Whole-DAG searches, blueprint_refs searches for semistable, Kummer tower, Cartan Leray, procyclic and torsor tower descent, and Lean-source searches preceded new-node creation. The procyclic theorem was found and reused. No new node restates a fundamental-class or purity clause; no item clause is merely discharged by a renamed lemma. All proof steps are under 2000 characters. citations.json was checked against the statements actually read, and off-unit generic tools are declared in split_report.tool_uses. The human's no-SGA route is retained.

## Resumed dry-run repair

The first resumed dry run exited with a citation-length diagnostic for Step 4. I reopened lem-amb-kummer-any-base and replaced the uninformative excerpt “is exact.” with its full statement, including the invertibility hypothesis and displayed Kummer sequence. Step 3 already verifies that ell is invertible on the root-cover base, and Step 4 uses this exact sequence for its connecting class. The proof and search records are retained.

I also corrected the inherited Not duplicates prose: the geometric node asserts Kummer-tower acyclicity, whereas the other new node asserts general torsor descent. The tokens 1207 and 1982 in the source scan are bibliographic identifiers, not Stacks tags. All pair entries are retained for the delivery checker. I reopened every statement cited in the six proof steps; the strict-local Cartesian square, compatibility of the class with strict localization, exact sections and stalk identification, normalized torsor H1 edge, and supported Kummer extension are explicitly present in those statements. The proof texts have no doubled TeX escapes and range from 590 to 1283 characters. The source extract at ILO XVI pp.250–251 confirms that the all-one case is the cited semistable input; the unavailable original proofs remain explicitly WANTED rather than being claimed as read.
